    \newsavebox\pandoc@box
    \newcommand*\pandocbounded[1]{%
      \sbox\pandoc@box{#1}%
      % scaling factors for width and height
      \Gscale@div\@tempa\textheight{\dimexpr\ht\pandoc@box+\dp\pandoc@box\relax}%
      \Gscale@div\@tempb\linewidth{\wd\pandoc@box}%
      % select the smaller of both
      \ifdim\@tempb\p@<\@tempa\p@
        \let\@tempa\@tempb
      \fi
      % scaling accordingly (\@tempa < 1)
      \ifdim\@tempa\p@<\p@
        \scalebox{\@tempa}{\usebox\pandoc@box}%
      % scaling not needed, use as it is
      \else
        \usebox{\pandoc@box}%
      \fi
    }

\def\PY@reset{\let\PY@it=\relax \let\PY@bf=\relax%

    % For linebreaks inside Verbatim environment from package fancyvrb.
    \makeatletter
        \newbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox
        \newbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox
        \newcommand*\Wrappedvisiblespace {\textcolor{red}{\textvisiblespace}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol {\textcolor{red}{\llap{\tiny$\m@th\hookrightarrow$}}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationindent {3ex }
        \newcommand*\Wrappedafterbreak {\kern\Wrappedcontinuationindent\copy\Wrappedcontinuationbox}
        % Take advantage of the already applied Pygments mark-up to insert
        % potential linebreaks for TeX processing.
        %        {, <, #, %, $, ' and ": go to next line.
        %        _, }, ^, &, >, - and ~: stay at end of broken line.
        % Use of \textquotesingle for straight quote.
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatspecials {%
            \def\PYGZus{\discretionary{\char`\_}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\_}}%
            \def\PYGZob{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\{}{\char`\{}}%
            \def\PYGZcb{\discretionary{\char`\}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\}}}%
            \def\PYGZca{\discretionary{\char`\^}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\^}}%
            \def\PYGZam{\discretionary{\char`\&}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\&}}%
            \def\PYGZlt{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\<}{\char`\<}}%
            \def\PYGZgt{\discretionary{\char`\>}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\>}}%
            \def\PYGZsh{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\#}{\char`\#}}%
            \def\PYGZpc{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\%}{\char`\%}}%
            \def\PYGZdl{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\$}{\char`\$}}%
            \def\PYGZhy{\discretionary{\char`\-}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\-}}%
            \def\PYGZsq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\textquotesingle}{\textquotesingle}}%
            \def\PYGZdq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\"}{\char`\"}}%
            \def\PYGZti{\discretionary{\char`\~}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\~}}%
        }
        % Some characters . , ; ? ! / are not pygmentized.
        % This macro makes them "active" and they will insert potential linebreaks
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatpunct {%
            \lccode`\~`\.\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\.}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\.}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\,\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\,}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\,}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\;\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\;}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\;}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\:\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\:}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\:}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\?\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\?}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\?}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\!\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\!}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\!}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\/\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\/}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\/}}}%
            \catcode`\.\active
            \catcode`\,\active
            \catcode`\;\active
            \catcode`\:\active
            \catcode`\?\active
            \catcode`\!\active
            \catcode`\/\active
            \lccode`\~`\~
        }
    \makeatother

    \let\OriginalVerbatim=\Verbatim
    \makeatletter
    \renewcommand{\Verbatim}[1][1]{%
        %\parskip\z@skip
        \sbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox {\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol}%
        \sbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox {\FV@SetupFont\Wrappedvisiblespace}%
        \def\FancyVerbFormatLine ##1{\hsize\linewidth
            \vtop{\raggedright\hyphenpenalty\z@\exhyphenpenalty\z@
                \doublehyphendemerits\z@\finalhyphendemerits\z@
                \strut ##1\strut}%
        }%
        % If the linebreak is at a space, the latter will be displayed as visible
        % space at end of first line, and a continuation symbol starts next line.
        % Stretch/shrink are however usually zero for typewriter font.
        \def\FV@Space {%
            \nobreak\hskip\z@ plus\fontdimen3\font minus\fontdimen4\font
            \discretionary{\copy\Wrappedvisiblespacebox}{\Wrappedafterbreak}
            {\kern\fontdimen2\font}%
        }%

        % Allow breaks at special characters using \PYG... macros.
        \Wrappedbreaksatspecials
        % Breaks at punctuation characters . , ; ? ! and / need catcode=\active
        \OriginalVerbatim[#1,codes*=\Wrappedbreaksatpunct]%
    }

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} substitui valores nulos/zero por epsilon (1e\PYZhy{}6), assegurando que todos os valores de RTT sejam positivos p/ boxcox}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} clip(lower=1e\PYZhy{}6) troca apenas zeros eventuais — não desloca a média.}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} boxcox devolve a série transformada e o λ ótimo; guardar o λ permite reverter ou aplicar em novos dados}
\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{apply\PYZus{}boxcox}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{col\PYZus{}in}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{rtt}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{col\PYZus{}out}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{bc\PYZus{}rtt}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 1) garante valores positivos}
    \PY{n}{rtt\PYZus{}pos} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col\PYZus{}in}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{clip}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{lower}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{1e\PYZhy{}6}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 2) aplica Box\PYZhy{}Cox}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col\PYZus{}out}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lam} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{boxcox}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{rtt\PYZus{}pos}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{lam}

            \PY{n}{linewidths}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{.6}\PY{p}{,}

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Correlation matrix (after dtype fix)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 1. Filtro de correlação (\PYZgt{} threshold)  ───────────────────────}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{CorrFilter}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{BaseEstimator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{TransformerMixin}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}Remove colunas com |ρ| \PYZgt{}= threshold (default 0.90).\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{threshold}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.90}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{threshold} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{threshold}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}drop\PYZus{}}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{None}
        
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{fit}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} matriz de correlação absoluta (apenas colunas numéricas)}
        \PY{n}{corr} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DataFrame}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{corr}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{abs}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{upper} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{corr}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{where}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{triu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ones}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{corr}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{k}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{bool}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}drop\PYZus{}} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{col} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{upper}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns}
                         \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{any}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{upper}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{threshold}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}
    
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{transform}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DataFrame}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}drop\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{errors}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ignore}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{values}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} para o SelectKBest saber o nº de features pós‑filtro}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{get\PYZus{}feature\PYZus{}names\PYZus{}out}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{names}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{names} \PY{o+ow}{is} \PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{k}{return} \PY{k+kc}{None}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{n} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{n} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{names} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{n} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}drop\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{]}

\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{CorrFilterBest}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{BaseEstimator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{TransformerMixin}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Para cada grupo de colunas com |ρ| ≥ threshold,}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    mantém a de maior F‑score ANOVA calculado no treino.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{threshold}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.90}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{threshold} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{threshold}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}drop\PYZus{}}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{None}          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} será preenchido no fit()}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{fit}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{} 1. F‑scores no subset de treino \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
        \PY{n}{F}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{f\PYZus{}classif}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y}\PY{p}{)}                       \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} retorna array}
        \PY{n}{F\PYZus{}scores} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Series}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{F}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{X}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{} 2. matriz de correlação \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
        \PY{n}{corr}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{X}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{corr}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{abs}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{mask}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{triu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ones}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{corr}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{k}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{bool}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{upper} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{corr}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{where}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mask}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{} 3. decidir quem remover \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
        \PY{n}{drop} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{col} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{upper}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{twins} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{upper}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{upper}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{threshold}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tolist}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{twins}\PY{p}{:}
                \PY{n}{group} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{+} \PY{n}{twins}          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} col + todos correlacionados ≥thr}
                \PY{n}{keep}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{F\PYZus{}scores}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{group}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{idxmax}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}   \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} maior F dentro do grupo}
                \PY{n}{drop}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{c} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{c} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{group} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{c} \PY{o}{!=} \PY{n}{keep}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}drop\PYZus{}} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{list}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{set}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{transform}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{X}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}drop\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{errors}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ignore}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fp} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{add\PYZus{}weight}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{name}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{fp}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{shape}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{num\PYZus{}classes}\PY{p}{,}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{initializer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{zeros}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dtype}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{dtype}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{update\PYZus{}state}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}true}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}pred}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{sample\PYZus{}weight}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 1) Função construtora do MLP}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} • `meta`   → dicionário que o SciKeras injeta com infos do fold}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}              (inclui o nº de atributos já pós‑pipeline).}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} • `l2\PYZus{}val` → fator de regularização L2 (weight‑decay).}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} • `drop`   → taxa de Dropout aplicada após cada camada densa.}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} • `n1/n2`  → nº de neurônios nas camadas ocultas 1 e 2.}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} O modelo compila em modo “sparse” (labels inteiros 0‑2) e}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} devolve rede pronta para ser embrulhada pelo KerasClassifier.}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{build\PYZus{}mlp}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{meta}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{l2\PYZus{}val}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n2}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backend}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{clear\PYZus{}session}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}                 \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} limpa grafos antigos}
    \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}inputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{meta}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{n\PYZus{}features\PYZus{}in\PYZus{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} nº de colunas que entram}
    \PY{n}{model} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Sequential}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Input}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}inputs}\PY{p}{,}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}                             \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} camada de entrada}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dense}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{activation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{relu}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}                          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} hidden‑1}
                              \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}regularizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{regularizers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{l2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{l2\PYZus{}val}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dropout}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dense}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{activation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{relu}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}                          \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} hidden‑2}
                              \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}regularizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{regularizers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{l2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{l2\PYZus{}val}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dropout}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dense}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{activation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{softmax}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}                        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} saída 3 classes}
    \PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{compile}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{adam}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}
                  \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{sparse\PYZus{}categorical\PYZus{}crossentropy}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}
                  \PY{n}{metrics}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{accuracy}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{F1Macro}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{num\PYZus{}classes}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} força a criação de um grafo concreto apenas UMA vez}
    \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}function} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{make\PYZus{}train\PYZus{}function}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}function}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{make\PYZus{}test\PYZus{}function}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{model}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 2) Wrapper SciKeras  → permite tunar hiper‑parâmetros Keras}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} callbacks \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
\PY{n}{early\PYZus{}stop} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{callbacks}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{EarlyStopping}\PY{p}{(}
               \PY{n}{patience}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{10}\PY{p}{,}                \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Treinamento para se val\PYZus{}loss não baixar por 10 épocas consecutivas}
               \PY{n}{restore\PYZus{}best\PYZus{}weights}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Após parar, volta aos pesos da época com menor val\PYZus{}loss (evita “over‑treinar”).}
               \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}monitor=\PYZdq{}val\PYZus{}accuracy\PYZdq{}, mode=\PYZdq{}max\PYZdq{},  \PYZsh{} já é calculado \PYZhy{} métrica observada é a acurácia (os 20 \PYZpc{} definidos por validation\PYZus{}split=0.20)}
               \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}monitor=\PYZdq{}val\PYZus{}loss\PYZdq{}, mode=\PYZdq{}min\PYZdq{},}
               \PY{n}{monitor}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{val\PYZus{}f1\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{mode}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{max}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} quanto menor a acuracia, melhor}
               \PY{n}{verbose} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)} 

\PY{n}{reduce\PYZus{}lr} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{callbacks}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ReduceLROnPlateau}\PY{p}{(}
               \PY{n}{monitor}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{val\PYZus{}loss}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}     \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ou \PYZdq{}val\PYZus{}accuracy\PYZdq{} / \PYZdq{}val\PYZus{}f1\PYZdq{}}
               \PY{n}{mode}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{min}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}             \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZdq{}min\PYZdq{} se métrica = loss, \PYZdq{}max\PYZdq{} se accuracy/F1}
               \PY{n}{factor}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.3}\PY{p}{,}             \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} novo\PYZus{}LR = LR\PYZus{}atual × 0.3}
               \PY{n}{patience}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{,}             \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} nº de épocas sem melhora}
               \PY{n}{min\PYZus{}lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{1e\PYZhy{}6}\PY{p}{,}            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} piso}
               \PY{n}{verbose}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{keras\PYZus{}clf} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{KerasClassifier}\PY{p}{(}
        \PY{n}{model}             \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{build\PYZus{}mlp}\PY{p}{,}            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} função acima}
        \PY{n}{epochs}            \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{200}\PY{p}{,}                  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} máx. de épocas}
        \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}        \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{16}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{verbose}           \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{validation\PYZus{}split}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.20}\PY{p}{,}                 \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 20 \PYZpc{} do treino p/ early‑stop}
        \PY{n}{callbacks}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{early\PYZus{}stop}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{reduce\PYZus{}lr}\PY{p}{]}       
\PY{p}{)}

\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 5‑fold estratificado (mesmo split usado em toda a otimização) \PYZhy{} mantém a proporção}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}Validar também com 10 folds}
\PY{n}{cv} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{StratifiedKFold}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}splits}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{shuffle}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{random\PYZus{}state}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{42}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 5) Busca aleatória (20 configurações × 5 folds = 100 treinamentos)}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}    • scoring = f1\PYZus{}macro  → balanceia classes}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}    • n\PYZus{}jobs = 1          → evita problemas de pickling com Keras}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}

\PY{n}{total} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ParameterGrid}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{param\PYZus{}dist}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}with parallel\PYZus{}backend(\PYZdq{}threading\PYZdq{}):            \PYZsh{} usa todos os núcleos sem pickling}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}GridSearch \PYZhy{} }
\PY{n}{rand} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{RandomizedSearchCV}\PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{estimator}            \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pipe}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{param\PYZus{}distributions}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{param\PYZus{}dist}\PY{p}{,}

    \PY{n}{cv}                   \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{cv}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{scoring}              \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{f1\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}jobs}               \PY{o}{=} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,}

    \PY{n}{verbose}              \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{random\PYZus{}state}         \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{42}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{7}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}construtora modelo}
\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{build\PYZus{}best}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{meta}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n1}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{96}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n2}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{32}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{l2\PYZus{}val}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{1e\PYZhy{}05}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{drop}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}inputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{meta}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{n\PYZus{}features\PYZus{}in\PYZus{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n}{model} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Sequential}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Input}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}inputs}\PY{p}{,}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dense}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{activation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{relu}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}regularizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{regularizers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{l2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{l2\PYZus{}val}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dropout}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dense}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{activation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{relu}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}regularizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{regularizers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{l2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{l2\PYZus{}val}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dropout}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dense}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{activation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{softmax}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{compile}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{adam}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{sparse\PYZus{}categorical\PYZus{}crossentropy}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{metrics}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{accuracy}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{model}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{8}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}Parametros de treino}
\PY{n}{best\PYZus{}clf} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{KerasClassifier}\PY{p}{(}
        \PY{n}{model}           \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{build\PYZus{}best}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{epochs}          \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}      \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{32}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{verbose}         \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{validation\PYZus{}split}\PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.2}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{callbacks}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{callbacks}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{EarlyStopping}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{patience}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{restore\PYZus{}best\PYZus{}weights}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{monitor}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{val\PYZus{}loss}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}
\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}pipeline para normalizar e depois modelar}
\PY{n}{final\PYZus{}pipe} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{Pipeline}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}
        \PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{scale}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{StandardScaler}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}(\PYZdq{}kbest\PYZdq{} , SelectKBest(f\PYZus{}classif, k=25)),}
        \PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{clf}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}  \PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{best\PYZus{}clf}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{} 3. Cross‑validation \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
\PY{n}{cv}      \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{StratifiedKFold}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}splits}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{shuffle}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{random\PYZus{}state}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{42}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{scoring} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{accuracy}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}       \PY{p}{:} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{accuracy}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}
           \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{precision\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{precision\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}
           \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{recall\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}   \PY{p}{:} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{recall\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}
           \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{f1\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}       \PY{p}{:} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{f1\PYZus{}macro}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{9}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} =============================================================}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{} 1. build\PYZus{}fn LSTM (usa meta) \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{build\PYZus{}lstm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{meta}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}classes}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}inputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{meta}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{n\PYZus{}features\PYZus{}in\PYZus{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}               \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ← nº de colunas após SelectKBest}
    \PY{n}{model} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Sequential}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}

        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Reshape}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}inputs}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}     \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 3‑D  [time‑steps=1, feats]}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LSTM}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{n}{tf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{keras}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layers}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dense}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}classes}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{activation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{softmax}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{compile}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{adam}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}
                  \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{sparse\PYZus{}categorical\PYZus{}crossentropy}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{model}

\PY{n}{clf} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{KerasClassifier}\PY{p}{(}

\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{} 2. Pipeline leakage‑free \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}

\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{} 1. build\PYZus{}fn para SimpleRNN \PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}
\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{build\PYZus{}rnn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{meta}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}classes}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
